This creates a structural double use: a converging force meets a preassembled force. The packet supplies a horse-speed image in the branch and a `جَمْع` as endpoint; I infer that the kinetic sequence concentrates the agents before impact. The object can remain the gathered group while also activating a mirror-image of gathered momentum on the agents' side.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_25864eb6a1e2ac93f6a5","text":"100:10-11 made \"جمع\" less exclusively martial: gathered army, gathered forum, and gathered account converged. 100:8 added the inward version, where severe love enters the middle of judgment, so the center-entry becomes psychological as well as spatial.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001646/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_2784e33aa54e335157bb","text":"1. **Peripheral force reaches an organized center.** `و س ط B003` supplies entering or being placed in the middle, and `ج م ع B002` supplies an assembled group or army. The prior sequence moves from running to ignition, raid, and obscuring dust; this ayah converts approach into interior position. The group is the structured target and the middle is the achieved location. The packet gives the path and endpoint; I infer that the earlier phases enable the breach. The line reads as completed penetration of an organized body, not simply proximity to a crowd.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B002","root_000259/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2c78c48ed450b52edb44","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:5:3\",\"root_000259\",[\"B010\",\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Girenler, toplanmış hedefe ulaşırken kendi hız ve güçlerini de tek bir hamlede yoğunlaştırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000130/B002","root_000259/B001","root_000259/B009"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_415471c7dd3346976841","text":"3. **Gathering is temporary and subject to analytic reversal.** `ج م ع B001` gathers scattered parts and `B009` marks wholeness without loss. Later, 100:9 activates `ب ع ث ر B002`, scattering and overturning contents, and 100:10 activates `ح ص ل B001`, recollecting a thing until its outcome is clear. The three operations form collect, disperse, and resolve. The packet supplies the operations; I infer that the latter two audit the first rather than merely follow it. `جَمْعًا` therefore reads as an aggregation whose apparent unity will be broken open and recomposed as a disclosed result.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000130/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_44ce82662ad24c347b29","text":"100:9-10 expose the gathered body's instability. `ب ع ث ر B002` disperses what had appeared whole, then `ح ص ل B001` recollects it as a resolved outcome. The surprise is that 100:5's culmination is also the starting material for a later audit: reaching the center does not settle the matter; it makes the aggregate available for disassembly and assessment.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B003","root_001646/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4bf86f0d01fee4ad83f4","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:5:1\",\"root_001646\",[\"B001\"]],[\"100:5:3\",\"root_000259\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Merkeze giriş, insanın dengeli yargı merkezinin mala şiddetli bağlılık tarafından işgal edilmesini de canlandırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B002","root_000993/B009","root_001119/B003","root_001646/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_4cfe90ccb36dbe8b4542","text":"1. **Penetrating the assembled center.** Evidence: و س ط B003 entering the middle; ج م ع B002 assembled group/army; ع د و B009 edge/bank; غ ي ر B003 altering state. Structural cue: packet motion progresses edge-run → ignition → morning incursion → dust → center-entry. Role: وسط is the tactical culmination; جمع is the target body. Abductive move: the prior flank/edge movement is completed by entry into the group's core. Reading change: the ayah reads as the moment the outside force becomes internal to the collective.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B002","root_001646/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_65e984a5f3723e1fbf64","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:5:1\",\"root_001646\",[\"B003\"]],[\"100:5:3\",\"root_000259\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Önceki kenar hareketi burada tamamlanır: dışarıdaki kuvvet örgütlü topluluğun içine ve merkezine yerleşir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_ad3f48c2663904ed7091","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"100:5:3\",\"root_000259\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Toplanan topluluk, tanıklığın ve gizli içeriğin delil olduğu ilerideki hesap meclisini önceden haber verir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B003","root_001646/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_d115f372177dec447167","text":"2. **Judicial center invaded by appetite.** Evidence: و س ط B001 just/choice balance; ج م ع B003 concerted resolve; later ك ن د B002 ingratitude, ح ب ب B002 love, ش د د B002 intensity. Structural cue: after the oaths, the moral subject is judged. Role: وسط supplies a norm of balanced middle; جمع supplies unified intention. Abductive move: the raid image activates a later moral model where intense desire penetrates and displaces the balanced center of judgment. Reading change: 100:5 becomes a psychological figure: the human's inward assembly is occupied by severe love of \"good.\"","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000259/B004","root_000822/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_b:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_fdc71b5596d7d549f7a9","text":"3. **Gathering as future court.** Evidence: ج م ع B004 gathering-place/day/summons; ش ه د B001 presence with witnessing and B002 testimony; ع ل م B001 knowledge; خ ب ر B001 tested knowledge.
